## Milestone 1A: PARTIAL (medium)

### Documented objectives / claims

- agrosphere-architecture/README.md:24: Milestone 1A estate implementation is recorded in docs/adr/0008-estate-management.md and docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md. Milestone 1B is recorded separately in ADR-0009.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/adr/0008-estate-management.md:3: Status: Accepted for the requested Milestone 1A implementation
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/architecture/INTEGRATION_PATTERNS.md:14: Milestone 1A update: estate publishes EstateCreated, EstateUpdated and PlantationBlockCreated/Updated using v2 routing and full entity snapshots (ADR-0008). V1 schemas are preserved; other producers remain v1 foundation designs. The estate relay is now implemented and container-tested. Spatial implements its v2 consumer in M1B; M1C verifies it with the real Estate publisher. V2 requires occurredAt/sourceService and retains explicit provenance and UUID identities. Retry budgets, retained FAILED records, replay and disabled-publication operation are documented in Estate Service README.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:13: Requirement sources: [milestone exit gates](../../roadmap/MILESTONES.md), [1A scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md), [1B scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1B.md), [1C scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md), [functional requirements](../../requirements/FUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md), [nonfunctional requirements](../../requirements/NONFUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md), and [ADR 0012 / 3A scope](../../adr/0012-rubber-tapping-operations.md). Original user-request transcripts and a separately approved 3B specification were not recovered. Milestone 2's retained completion report documents functional DoD items 1–17 but does not reproduce the original full numbered specification; assessment uses the recoverable roadmap gate, implementation and report.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:33: | 1A compatible contracts and packaging | Estate v2 namespace preserves incompatible v1; independent Maven/Docker | [1A report](../../../../agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md) records actual serialized-event and all-16-operation validation, Java 21/container image execution; later Java 25 evidence supersedes any assumption of a single universal Java target |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:3: Implementation resides only in agrosphere-estate-service, with coordinated contracts 0.2.0 and ADR-0008. No Milestone 1B implementation is included.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:5: - [x] Pure domain models, DTOs, ports/adapters and service-owned migrations
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:6: - [x] Estate/division/block/clone REST API and paginated scoped queries
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:7: - [x] Validated JWT issuer/audience/roles/estate scope
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:8: - [x] Atomic outbox, confirmed RabbitMQ delivery, retries and replay guidance
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:9: - [x] Domain/security/messaging tests and PostgreSQL/RabbitMQ integration checks
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:10: - [x] V1 event preservation and additive v2 schemas/OpenAPI
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:11: - [x] Reproducible runbook and synthetic example requests
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:12: - [x] Java 21 verification: 47 tests passed, no skips
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:13: - [x] Docker image built successfully; see service completion report
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:15: Runtime verification results and limitations are recorded in the service's docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md, not inferred from implementation checkboxes. Central infrastructure and all other service repositories remain unchanged.
- agrosphere-contracts/README.md:7: See AGENTS.md for scope and engineering rules. Estate Service now defines Milestone 1A business operations; Spatial Service defines Milestone 1B; other service APIs remain foundation placeholders. Architecture lives in the sibling agrosphere-architecture repository; versioned API/event definitions live in agrosphere-contracts. Do not add path dependencies to sibling projects.
- agrosphere-contracts/README.md:17: Consumer transaction: insert (consumer_name,event_id) with ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING; apply projection only when inserted, in the same transaction; commit before ACK. Publisher writes outbox with domain mutation; relay publishes persistent messages with confirms and marks published only after confirmation. Duplicates are expected. Bounded retries and DLQ handling are required before consumers ship. Estate Service implements its v2 relay in Milestone 1A; consumer handlers remain separate work. Validate forecast period ordering and business semantics in applications as JSON Schema cannot enforce them.
- agrosphere-contracts/README.md:21: Estate OpenAPI now defines sixteen business operations and Problem Details, JWT scope, pagination and optimistic versions. Four new self-contained events/v2 snapshot schemas use occurredAt/sourceService, canonical estateId/entityId and optional blockId as applicable. Every v1 contract remains byte-for-byte preserved. V1 EstateCreated requires companyId, which M1A does not own; v2 avoids inventing that identity. V1 block update fields cannot express status/count changes. Estate Service publishes only v2; existing v1 consumers must explicitly migrate to agrosphere.<EventType>.v2. There is no dual publishing.
- agrosphere-estate-service/AGENTS.md:5: Current scope: Milestone 1A Estate plus requested Milestone 1C integration patches only; preserve all existing business rules, contracts and persistence ownership.
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:7: Estate Service is implemented, independently buildable and container-tested. All requested acceptance checks completed locally: Java 21 build/tests, PostgreSQL startup, fresh and upgrade Flyway migrations, REST APIs, JWT authorization, atomic outbox creation, real RabbitMQ publication, Docker image and reproducible instructions. No other microservice was implemented, no central infrastructure configuration changed, no remote/commit/push/cloud deployment occurred, and Milestone 1B was not started.
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:11: ## Implemented APIs
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:13: All routes use /api/v1, bearer JWTs and JSON. Collections support status, page and size, stable ID ordering and SQL-scoped pagination. Creation returns 201; resource commands support validation and Problem Details. No physical-delete API exists.
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:15: | Resource | Operations |
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:16: |---|---|
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:17: | Estates | POST/GET /estates; GET/PUT /estates/{id}; PATCH /estates/{id}/status |
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:18: | Divisions | POST/GET /estates/{estateId}/divisions; GET/PUT /divisions/{id} |
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:19: | Blocks | POST /divisions/{divisionId}/blocks; GET /estates/{estateId}/blocks; GET/PUT /blocks/{id}; PATCH /blocks/{id}/status |
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:20: | Clones | POST/GET /rubber-clones |
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:38: Domain writes and mandatory outbox inserts share one JPA-managed database transaction. The dispatcher locks due rows with SKIP LOCKED and sends persistent mandatory messages to durable agrosphere.domain using publisher confirms. Returns, nacks and exceptions retry with configurable exponential backoff; exhausted events remain FAILED for operator-controlled replay. Publication defaults disabled while preserving outbox records. Confirmation/crash ambiguity permits duplicates with the same eventId. Consumers must deduplicate consumer_name/eventId transactionally, ACK after commit and reject stale revisions. No exactly-once delivery is claimed. The implementation follows the [Spring AMQP confirm/return API](https://docs.spring.io/spring-amqp/reference/amqp/template.html).

## Milestone 1B: PARTIAL (medium)

### Documented objectives / claims

- agrosphere-architecture/README.md:24: Milestone 1A estate implementation is recorded in docs/adr/0008-estate-management.md and docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md. Milestone 1B is recorded separately in ADR-0009.
- agrosphere-architecture/README.md:26: Requested Milestone 1B spatial implementation is recorded in docs/adr/0009-spatial-digital-twin.md and docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1B.md. Spatial Service targets Java 21 as explicitly requested; Estate retains its existing Java 25 build. Milestone 1C local integration is recorded in ADR-0010/0011 and docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/adr/0009-spatial-digital-twin.md:3: Status: Accepted for the requested Milestone 1B implementation
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/architecture/INTEGRATION_PATTERNS.md:14: Milestone 1A update: estate publishes EstateCreated, EstateUpdated and PlantationBlockCreated/Updated using v2 routing and full entity snapshots (ADR-0008). V1 schemas are preserved; other producers remain v1 foundation designs. The estate relay is now implemented and container-tested. Spatial implements its v2 consumer in M1B; M1C verifies it with the real Estate publisher. V2 requires occurredAt/sourceService and retains explicit provenance and UUID identities. Retry budgets, retained FAILED records, replay and disabled-publication operation are documented in Estate Service README.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:13: Requirement sources: [milestone exit gates](../../roadmap/MILESTONES.md), [1A scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md), [1B scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1B.md), [1C scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md), [functional requirements](../../requirements/FUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md), [nonfunctional requirements](../../requirements/NONFUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md), and [ADR 0012 / 3A scope](../../adr/0012-rubber-tapping-operations.md). Original user-request transcripts and a separately approved 3B specification were not recovered. Milestone 2's retained completion report documents functional DoD items 1–17 but does not reproduce the original full numbered specification; assessment uses the recoverable roadmap gate, implementation and report.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:35: | 1B event idempotency and ordering | [EstateProjection.java](../../../../agrosphere-spatial-service/src/main/java/com/agrosphere/spatial/adapter/in/messaging/EstateProjection.java) inserts consumer/event marker and updates only newer revisions within one transaction | Spatial IT + 1C duplicate/out-of-order/late-parent acceptance group; [1B report](../../../../agrosphere-spatial-service/docs/MILESTONE_1B_REPORT.md) records real broker, DLQ and schema checks |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONES.md:15: Milestone 1B is authorized by its separate request and implemented in Spatial Service; see MILESTONE_1B.md and ADR-0009. This does not authorize Milestone 1C.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md:3: Implementation resides only in agrosphere-estate-service, with coordinated contracts 0.2.0 and ADR-0008. No Milestone 1B implementation is included.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1B.md:3: Requested implementation: independent Spatial Service 0.2.0; independent contracts 0.3.0. See ADR-0009 and the spatial repository docs/MILESTONE_1B_REPORT.md for concrete source, schema, endpoints, actual test results, run commands and limitations.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1B.md:5: Delivered scope includes WGS84 features/history, spheroidal areas, bounded spatial queries, GeoJSON import/export, layers/assets, estate v2 projections, spatial v1 events/outbox, estate-scoped JWT policy and independent deployment artifacts. All fixtures are SIMULATED and require no deployed Estate Service.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/NEXT_CODEX_PROMPT.md:3: Implement AgroSphere Milestone 1B Spatial Service and estate identity projection only. Read affected AGENTS.md files, architecture ADR-0008, the completed Estate Service report, infrastructure configuration and contracts 0.2.0 first. Preserve independent Git roots and service-owned databases. Consume the Estate Service v2 snapshots with transactional consumer_name/eventId deduplication, revision-aware updates, retry/DLQ/replay and reconciliation. Implement spatial-owned PostGIS geometry and RFC 7946 WGS84 API contracts, using canonical estate/block UUIDs without cross-database access. Reject new operational use of inactive blocks and label synthetic fixtures SIMULATED. Add contract/security/PostGIS integration and duplicate/out-of-order event tests. Validate builds, migrations, permissions and Docker, document actual results and blockers, and stop after Milestone 1B. Do not add operations, telemetry, AI, web/immersive functionality or deploy to Azure.
- agrosphere-contracts/README.md:7: See AGENTS.md for scope and engineering rules. Estate Service now defines Milestone 1A business operations; Spatial Service defines Milestone 1B; other service APIs remain foundation placeholders. Architecture lives in the sibling agrosphere-architecture repository; versioned API/event definitions live in agrosphere-contracts. Do not add path dependencies to sibling projects.
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:7: Estate Service is implemented, independently buildable and container-tested. All requested acceptance checks completed locally: Java 21 build/tests, PostgreSQL startup, fresh and upgrade Flyway migrations, REST APIs, JWT authorization, atomic outbox creation, real RabbitMQ publication, Docker image and reproducible instructions. No other microservice was implemented, no central infrastructure configuration changed, no remote/commit/push/cloud deployment occurred, and Milestone 1B was not started.
- agrosphere-estate-service/docs/MILESTONE_1A_REPORT.md:185: Implement AgroSphere Milestone 1B Spatial Service and estate identity projection only. Read affected AGENTS.md files, architecture ADR-0008, the completed Estate Service report, infrastructure configuration and contracts 0.2.0 first. Preserve independent Git roots and service-owned databases. Consume the Estate Service v2 snapshots with transactional consumer_name/eventId deduplication, revision-aware updates, retry/DLQ/replay and reconciliation. Implement spatial-owned PostGIS geometry and RFC 7946 WGS84 API contracts, using canonical estate/block UUIDs without cross-database access. Reject new operational use of inactive blocks and label synthetic fixtures SIMULATED. Add contract/security/PostGIS integration and duplicate/out-of-order event tests. Validate builds, migrations, permissions and Docker, document actual results and blockers, and stop after Milestone 1B. Do not add operations, telemetry, AI, web/immersive functionality or deploy to Azure.
- agrosphere-spatial-service/AGENTS.md:5: Current scope: Milestone 1B Spatial plus requested Milestone 1C integration patches only; preserve GIS semantics, event projection, contracts and persistence ownership.
- agrosphere-spatial-service/docs/MILESTONE_1B_REPORT.md:7: The detailed inventory below covers service implementation, contract snapshots, sample fixtures, deployment and architecture updates. The original Application.java was renamed SpatialServiceApplication.java to avoid the Windows case-insensitive class/package collision with application/. Maven Wrapper and V1 migration remain unchanged. Estate Service and Infrastructure source files were not modified.

- agrosphere-spatial-service/docs/MILESTONE_1B_REPORT.md:17: | reference_entity | Minimal canonical IDs, parent/estate membership, lifecycle and revision; inferred division marker |

## Milestone 1C: PARTIAL (medium)

### Documented objectives / claims

- agrosphere-api-gateway/AGENTS.md:5: Current scope: Milestone 1C gateway integration: routing, JWT, CORS, correlation, timeouts, local rate limiting, observability and tests. No plantation business logic.
- agrosphere-architecture/AGENTS.md:5: Current scope: Architecture through requested Milestone 1C; document local integration decisions and actual validation. No service code here.
- agrosphere-architecture/README.md:3: Architecture and implementation decisions through Milestone 1C for the AgroSphere Rubber Plantation Digital Twin.
- agrosphere-architecture/README.md:26: Requested Milestone 1B spatial implementation is recorded in docs/adr/0009-spatial-digital-twin.md and docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1B.md. Spatial Service targets Java 21 as explicitly requested; Estate retains its existing Java 25 build. Milestone 1C local integration is recorded in ADR-0010/0011 and docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/adr/0010-local-platform-integration.md:3: Status: Accepted for the requested Milestone 1C implementation
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/architecture/INTEGRATION_PATTERNS.md:12: JWT issuer is configured without embedded secrets. Audience/estate-role enforcement and security regression tests are mandatory before domain routes. M1C implements gateway routing and a bounded per-process authenticated-subject limiter; ADR-0010 records the local-only policy. OpenTelemetry spans use W3C trace context and correlation IDs without logging credentials.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/architecture/INTEGRATION_PATTERNS.md:14: Milestone 1A update: estate publishes EstateCreated, EstateUpdated and PlantationBlockCreated/Updated using v2 routing and full entity snapshots (ADR-0008). V1 schemas are preserved; other producers remain v1 foundation designs. The estate relay is now implemented and container-tested. Spatial implements its v2 consumer in M1B; M1C verifies it with the real Estate publisher. V2 requires occurredAt/sourceService and retains explicit provenance and UUID identities. Retry budgets, retained FAILED records, replay and disabled-publication operation are documented in Estate Service README.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:13: Requirement sources: [milestone exit gates](../../roadmap/MILESTONES.md), [1A scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md), [1B scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1B.md), [1C scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md), [functional requirements](../../requirements/FUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md), [nonfunctional requirements](../../requirements/NONFUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md), and [ADR 0012 / 3A scope](../../adr/0012-rubber-tapping-operations.md). Original user-request transcripts and a separately approved 3B specification were not recovered. Milestone 2's retained completion report documents functional DoD items 1–17 but does not reproduce the original full numbered specification; assessment uses the recoverable roadmap gate, implementation and report.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:36: | 1C identity, isolation, data flow and fault recovery | [smoke_platform.py](../../../../agrosphere-infrastructure/scripts/smoke_platform.py); service-owned DBs; signed trusted roles/scopes; Gateway | [platform-smoke.json](../../../../agrosphere-infrastructure/target/platform-smoke.json) and [full-smoke log](../../../../agrosphere-infrastructure/milestone-1c-full-smoke.log) retain 12 groups, including direct backend denial, cross-DB denial, service outage and broker recovery |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:37: | 1C trace propagation and local packaging | Persisted trace transport, Collector/Jaeger, health-gated Compose | Same platform artifacts explicitly record HTTP→outbox→Rabbit→consumer spans; [1C report](../../../../agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md) records independent builds and contract validation |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONES.md:15: Milestone 1B is authorized by its separate request and implemented in Spatial Service; see MILESTONE_1B.md and ADR-0009. This does not authorize Milestone 1C.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONES.md:17: Milestone 1C local integration is implemented; see MILESTONE_1C.md, ADR-0010 and the infrastructure completion report for executed evidence. Client implementation remains future scope.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md:3: Implemented in independent Infrastructure 0.2.0, Gateway 0.2.0, Estate 0.2.1 and Spatial 0.2.1 repositories. Architecture release 0.4.0 records ADRs 0010/0011; Contracts 0.3.1 documents compatibility with unchanged 0.3.0 schemas.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md:5: The local apps profile integrates real Keycloak, RabbitMQ, isolated service databases/PostGIS, gateway routes/JWT/CORS/limits, health dependencies and Collector/Jaeger tracing. Automated shared-platform acceptance verifies canonical Estate/Spatial integration, trusted role/estate scope, actual API/event contracts, database isolation, duplicate event handling and service/broker outage recovery. Infrastructure README provides run commands and private environment initialization. See infrastructure docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md for actual results and limitations.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md:7: Client implementation, authoritative division lifecycle, production deployment/identity/TLS, retained observability and performance/backup SLAs remain subsequent work. No remote publication or cloud resources were created.
- agrosphere-contracts/AGENTS.md:5: Current scope: Estate/Spatial contracts through Milestone 1C compatibility verification. Preserve existing schema bytes and version namespaces; tracing metadata belongs to transport, not a silent event-envelope change.
- agrosphere-estate-service/AGENTS.md:5: Current scope: Milestone 1A Estate plus requested Milestone 1C integration patches only; preserve all existing business rules, contracts and persistence ownership.
- agrosphere-estate-service/README.md:98: Milestone 1C additionally enables Rabbit producer/consumer observations and persists W3C transport context with atomic outbox rows, restoring it before confirmed publication. Event JSON is unchanged; old rows with null context remain publishable. The central acceptance suite asserts HTTP-to-relay-to-consumer trace continuity in Jaeger. JSON bodies are bounded to 2 MiB (including streamed bodies); infrastructure injects a distinct service field into structured logs. The full PKCE/two-division/ten-block canonical GeoJSON test and safe no-reset procedures are in infrastructure docs/INTEGRATION_TESTING.md and LOCAL_DEVELOPMENT.md.
- agrosphere-infrastructure/AGENTS.md:5: Current scope: Milestone 1C shared local infrastructure, identity, broker/tracing configuration and platform acceptance. Preserve service-owned migrations and independent application builds.
- agrosphere-infrastructure/CHANGELOG.md:11: Milestone 1C central Compose apps profile, real Keycloak/PKCE and trusted claims, broker topology, Collector/Jaeger, private environment setup, pinned contract acceptance/recovery tests and independent-image CI.
- agrosphere-infrastructure/README.md:95: Pinned response/event snapshots under tests/contracts are from contracts release 0.3.0 (Estate API/events retain 0.2.0 semantics); SHA-256 manifest guards accidental drift. No runtime/build dependency reads sibling contracts. Each Java repository runs its own Maven/JWT/integration/contract checks. CI validates infrastructure and builds the two observability images. The manually dispatched platform workflow takes independently published application image references and runs acceptance without sibling checkouts. Registry images/remotes were not published or created here. See docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md for actual local results, baseline evidence, repository changes and limitations.
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:3: Date: 2026-10-24 (America/Chicago). Final scope: the complete requirements in the user's follow-up, including sections 6–19. This report supersedes the earlier partial-attachment report.
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:7: All sixteen definition-of-done criteria are implemented and locally verified. Gateway 0.2.0, Estate 0.2.1, Spatial 0.2.1 and infrastructure 0.2.0 run as independently built images. Architecture 0.4.0 records integration decisions. Contracts 0.3.1 adds compatibility documentation only; API/event schema snapshots remain pinned to unchanged release 0.3.0 (Estate API/v2 semantics from 0.2.0). The stack remains running and healthy.
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:13: ## Preparation and implementation sequence
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:15: Read infrastructure AGENTS/README, all existing ADRs, architecture/requirements/diagrams, contracts and event versions, Estate/Spatial completion reports and implementation, gateway scaffold, environment/certificate inventory, service migrations, Compose/overlays and all affected CI workflows before modifying their repositories. No reusable local certificate files existed. Existing implementation was verified rather than assumed complete.
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:17: The initial inspection/plan selected reuse of isolated databases, existing outboxes/projection and compatible Gateway MVC. Baseline builds/contracts were recorded before configuration work. Implementation then connected identity, broker topology and gateway, added health-gated orchestration/HTTP tracing, executed initial integration and fixed discovered issues. When the full request arrived, it added real PKCE users, the exact ten-block GeoJSON scenario, durable outbox trace context, direct backend/request-size assertions, dedicated runbooks and full reporting. No business service was replaced or expanded beyond M1C.
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:19: | Baseline repository | Actually executed before implementation |
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:20: |---|---|
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:21: | Infrastructure | Existing all-profile Compose parse passed; existing seven-database/non-superuser bootstrap inspected |
- agrosphere-infrastructure/docs/MILESTONE_1C_REPORT.md:22: | Gateway | Maven verify: 4 security tests passed |

## Milestone 2: PARTIAL (medium)

### Documented objectives / claims

- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-discovery.md:5: Operations is Boot 4.0.9, Java 25, JUnit 5.14.4, Maven wrapper; only authenticated foundation routes, V1 outbox and processed-event tables exist. No tapping domain or relay exists. Estate has UUID REST master data, timezone, block estateId, scoped realm-role authorization, issuer/audience JWT validation and a confirmed outbox. Spatial has service-owned PostGIS migrations and Estate event projection. Gateway currently routes Estate and Spatial only. Infrastructure supplies isolated service databases, RabbitMQ topic exchange agrosphere.domain, Keycloak trusted estate_ids and realm_access.roles, apps profile and OTLP. Web is Angular/Cesium Milestone 2; remaining services are foundation scaffolds. Each repository retains independent build/deploy configuration.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:5: **Result:** local delivery of 1A, 1B, 1C and the tapping-only 3A scope is supported by code and historical execution evidence. **Milestone 2 remains PARTIAL because its performance exit gate is open. The next eligible acceptance work is Milestone 2 performance closure. 3B is the next feature-planning candidate, not an authorized implementation.** No documented dependency requires Milestone 2 rendering performance for backend-only 3B planning; a strict sequential declaration of all prior gates complete must wait for that closure.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:13: Requirement sources: [milestone exit gates](../../roadmap/MILESTONES.md), [1A scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1A.md), [1B scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1B.md), [1C scope](../../roadmap/MILESTONE_1C.md), [functional requirements](../../requirements/FUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md), [nonfunctional requirements](../../requirements/NONFUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md), and [ADR 0012 / 3A scope](../../adr/0012-rubber-tapping-operations.md). Original user-request transcripts and a separately approved 3B specification were not recovered. Milestone 2's retained completion report documents functional DoD items 1–17 but does not reproduce the original full numbered specification; assessment uses the recoverable roadmap gate, implementation and report.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:39: | 2 performance | requestRenderMode is an optimization, not measured acceptance | [Milestone 2 acceptance limits](../../../../agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md) explicitly leaves reference-device FPS and initial useful estate view unmeasured. Roadmap requires verified frame budget |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:50: 5. **Broad requirements remain open.** Company identity in FR-01 is not implemented by the narrowed 1A scope. Authoritative division lifecycle is absent. The proposed metadata p95 <500 ms at 20 concurrent users has no recovered benchmark; NONFUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS calls these hypotheses, not achieved SLAs. Record this as M1-wide nonfunctional debt, not a fabricated pass or an automatic retroactive failure of every specific 1A–1C local exit gate. Reference-device visualization performance is an explicit M2 exit gate and does block full M2 acceptance.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:56: **First: close Milestone 2 acceptance.** Agree a named reference desktop/GPU, browser/version, representative estate and geometry/asset budget. Record repeatable cold/warm useful-estate-view times and interactive frame measurements during navigation, selection and layer toggles; retain browser/provider/configuration details, sample count and results against the agreed minimum 30 FPS target. The web report mentions a 10-second initial-view target; the original approved timing criterion was not recovered, so confirm that budget before treating it as a hard acceptance threshold. Choose a representative permitted terrain/imagery configuration and state which provider/fallback paths were exercised. Attach evidence and rerun the relevant functional checks before declaring full M2 acceptance. This audit does not implement or perform that work.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/reconciliation-1a-3a/ACCEPTANCE_MATRIX.md:60: There is no recovered explicit dependency making M2 GPU performance a prerequisite for backend-only 3B planning. Accordingly, 3B planning can be eligible while M2 remains partial; advancing a strict sequential acceptance baseline or claiming all milestones through 3A fully accepted cannot. Fresh current-revision validation is still needed for a new formal acceptance/implementation checkpoint. This report leaves the existing 1A review candidate and approval state untouched.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/requirements/NONFUNCTIONAL_REQUIREMENTS.md:9: | Visualization | Proposed 30 fps minimum on a named reference desktop; streaming/LOD and assets budget set in M2/M5 |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/roadmap/IMPLEMENTATION_BACKLOG.md:13: | P1 M2-01 | Cesium asset/worker pipeline, terrain and block selection | web | Licensing, provenance, IDs, source accuracy and performance measured |
- agrosphere-web/AGENTS.md:3: This independent Git repository implements Milestone 2: Angular/CesiumJS browser digital twin. Preserve independent build, release, CI and Docker deployment. Do not create a parent repository, submodule, sibling runtime dependency, backend/BFF, shared persistence model, cloud deployment or new service endpoint.
- agrosphere-web/CHANGELOG.md:5: - Implement Angular Milestone 2 shell, guarded routing, Keycloak Code/PKCE session, memory token renewal and Gateway-only authorization.
- agrosphere-web/docs/INTEGRATION.md:27: - The web scaffold's AGENTS scope was Milestone 0; the explicit Milestone 2 request supersedes it, and web instructions now reflect this implementation.
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:3: Date: October 24, 2026 (America/Chicago). Web release: 0.2.0. Work is confined to agrosphere-web; backend source, API contracts in owning repositories, data ownership, and independent deployment remain intact. No Git commit/push, remote repository creation, cloud resource or Milestone 3 implementation was performed.
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:5: ## A. Implementation summary
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:7: Implemented a standalone Angular enterprise shell with responsive/collapsible navigation, breadcrumbs, estate name/code search and canonical selection, authenticated user/logout controls, empty/loading/error/retry states and keyboard-accessible forms.
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:15: Decisions follow the existing architecture: direct Cesium integration, local Signals plus RxJS cancellation, generated OpenAPI types and small adapters, shell-scoped data, runtime public configuration, no NgRx/BFF/backend coupling.
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:21: - Estate GET /estates; /estates/{estateId}/divisions; /estates/{estateId}/blocks; /blocks/{id}.
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:22: - Spatial GET /spatial/estates/{estateId}/features; /layers; /assets.
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:23: - Typed adapters also expose current block geometry and POST point query; the viewer reuses loaded geometry to avoid redundant selection requests.
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:31: Default terrain is the offline ellipsoid; imagery defaults to none. Runtime configuration supports custom imagery templates, custom terrain, optional scoped public ion tokens/world terrain and explicit attribution. Cesium attribution remains visible. Provider failures fall back to ellipsoid/no imagery; unavailable WebGL retains searchable metadata and details.
- agrosphere-web/docs/MILESTONE_2_REPORT.md:41: The existing agrosphere realm / agrosphere-web public client uses Authorization Code with PKCE S256, openid + agrosphere-api scopes, and issuer-administered roles/estate_ids. Tokens stay in adapter memory and renew before Gateway calls/on expiration. Direct login initializes the adapter; logout retains its ID-token end-session hint until redirect.

### Documented objectives / claims

- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:3: Status: COMPLETE — implemented and verified locally on 2026-10-24. No commit, push, remote CI run or shared-environment deployment was performed. Milestone 3B was not started.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:7: Rubber tapping is implemented in the independently buildable Operations service, from draft schedules through bounded, duplicate-safe dated assignments, owned-worker execution, missed reasons, cancellations, live summaries and atomic outbox events. PostgreSQL migrations and real RabbitMQ publication/retry have executed successfully. A disposable full Compose stack verified real Keycloak PKCE, Gateway routing, Estate HTTP validation, scoped worker execution, auditor denial, summaries and confirmed events.

- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:22: ## Implemented domain and persistence
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:32: | Method | Path | Behavior |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:33: |---|---|---|
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:34: | POST / GET | /schedules | Create (201) / scoped page |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:35: | GET / PATCH | /schedules/{id} | Read / replace draft editable configuration |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:36: | POST | /schedules/{id}/activate | Activate or resume |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:37: | POST | /schedules/{id}/suspend | Stop generation |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:38: | POST | /schedules/{id}/close | Final schedule closure |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:39: | POST | /schedules/{id}/generate-assignments | Bounded anchored recurrence, permanent occurrence deduplication |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:40: | POST / GET | /assignments | Authorized ad hoc creation (201) / scoped filter page |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:41: | GET | /assignments/{id} | Assignment plus execution/missed detail |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:42: | PATCH | /assignments/{id}/worker | Reassign PLANNED work |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:43: | POST | /assignments/{id}/start | Create execution and begin |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:44: | POST | /assignments/{id}/complete | Validate actual trees and finalize |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:45: | POST | /assignments/{id}/mark-missed | Required reason, today/past estate-local date |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:46: | POST | /assignments/{id}/cancel | Management-only immutable cancellation |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:47: | GET | /records | Scoped paginated execution records |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:48: | GET | /summary | Total, worker or block counts/trees; grouped pagination |
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:60: Issuer/audience/cryptographic JWT validation follows the existing platform. Roles and trusted estate_ids are enforced inside Operations for individual objects and collection queries. PLATFORM_ADMIN/PLANTATION_ADMIN are global; ESTATE_ADMIN/ESTATE_MANAGER manage scoped schedules and assignments; FIELD_SUPERVISOR manages scoped assignments; FIELD_WORKER executes only own scoped assignments using signed sub; AUDITOR/VIEWER read only. Additional read roles do not remove a worker's own execution permission or grant writes to others.
- agrosphere-architecture/docs/milestones/milestone-3a-completion.md:62: Operations delegates the authenticated bearer token to the real Estate GET estate/block APIs, using bounded timeouts, no redirects, canonical timezone, active RUBBER ownership checks and explicit denial/unavailability mapping. No external database access or fabricated success fallback exists. Estate operational roles receive scoped master-data reads only; existing master-data write permissions remain enforced.
